- 2026-07-15T08:32:50.949Z i1-i2-core-project-context-foundation-rootcause2-p1 → AlembicCore — AlembicCore PC-F root-cause re-derivation combined package。原任务 recurringProblem=true，本包不是第三次普通 point-fix；必须用新的根因模型收敛 controller 在 commit 5414d681c1fb1a34578d4f55fa829115364900fe 上独立复现的两项缺陷。只允许修改 AlembicCore 对应 foundation/audit/tests/evidence；不进入 I3+，不修改 Alembic/Plugin/Agent/Dashboard，不启动 Test，不重做已通过的 store/cache 设计，不修 ASTChunker。

根因假设 A — ownership namespace 不完整：
- 当前 audit 的 directory ownerModuleIds 与真实 import/package namespace 脱节；MR repositories 未提供 @alembic/* package/export aliases，classifier 又不能识别 current repo self-package imports，造成至少 170 条已认证范围内依赖被归为 expected-external。
- 重新建立可审计的 canonical dependency ownership：aliases/exports 必须从真实 package metadata或显式、版本化、逐 repo 冻结的 ownership 输入派生，绑定 repoId/module owner，不得按字符串前缀把所有 @alembic/* 放行。
- current repo self-package/subpath 必须归入 declared internal；若 ProjectContext 仍无法用真实 seeds/ownership解析，必须是 confirmed defect并让 readiness/openConfirmedDefects失败。已批准 sibling repo 必须标出 relatedRepoId并守恒，真正第三方才可 expected-external。
- MR 五仓与 SP root+4 均重新生成九请求矩阵；报告给出 internal-resolved、approved-sibling、expected-external、confirmed-defect 的 counts/samples/ownership evidence。禁止仅修改标签而不证明 request output、module seeds和dependency graph可用。

根因假设 B — pre-read revision 不能认证 read content：
- 当前每轮 captureRepositories 只在读文件前 observeRevision；重复两次同样流程仍允许 source 在两轮 observe 后、read 中变更，最终签出 git-clean commit/tree + dirty blob 且 readiness=passed。
- 设计并实现真正的 coherent snapshot protocol。每轮必须有读取前后 revision/content fence；git-clean 声明还必须证明被读取 eligible bytes 与所声明 tree一致，不能只比较两次 pre-read commit/tree。dirty/content 模式必须把最终完整 content hash与稳定观察绑定。capture 结束后只能认证一个有明确边界的状态。
- 将 controller 对抗探针加入 RED/GREEN：两次 observe 均返回同 clean revision、两次 read 均切入同一 dirty bytes、最终 dirty；修复后必须抛 PROJECT_CONTEXT_SOURCE_STATE_DRIFT。另覆盖 transient clean→dirty→clean、add/delete/modify、dirty→dirty 和 abort，避免把取消误报 drift。
- 若需要扩展 host-port observation contract，保持公共子路径向后兼容或提供显式版本迁移；不得把锁或宿主专用 Git 实现硬编码进纯 Core contract。

证据与验证：
- 在 5414d681… 上保留这两个 controller probe 的 failing-before，提交一个新的清晰 repair commit。
- 重跑 build:check、focused suite、完整 npm test、public API/layer/consumer import/scope/smoke/output-budget/space/naming/Biome/retired-symbol gates；npm run check 的 ASTChunker 基线只可用不变 hash waiver，不得修改。
- 重新运行双项目 fresh-process audit和 parent loaded-artifact reproduction；报告不得以 openConfirmedDefects=[] 掩盖任何 internal/sibling 未解析项。
- 两阶段自审必须先逐条回答根因 A/B，再审 compatibility、TOCTOU/failure/replay、性能与报告诚实性。返回 parent/commit/tree、diff、命令、报告 hash、逐项 probe 输出和剩余风险。
- 返回 TargetResultEnvelope 只触发 controller 最后一轮同根因复验；不解锁下游。 (intent: 放弃“已有 alias 分类与双快照足够”的旧假设，重新证明 ProjectContext 依赖归属和 capture 原子边界：package/module alias 必须来自可审计 ownership，clean revision 必须与被签名内容属于同一稳定状态。)

1. 在现有 DatabaseConnection 单源生命周期内建立可证明不变量：一个 DatabaseConnection 同时最多拥有一个活动 native SQLite handle。推荐最小实现是在已有活动 handle 未经 `close()` 释放时，重复 `connect()` 以稳定错误 fail closed；若选择逐 native handle 登记，必须同样简洁并证明不存在不可达 handle。不得新建 store/service/DB manager。

- 2026-07-17T01:12:26.684Z i8r-alembic-main-two-stage-quiesced-observation-redesign2-p1 → Alembic — 同一 demand 的 Alembic NEXT combined package。以 clean baseline Alembic@43306622a06e2782381aa1aa257681f1f7aa8781 为父提交，完整 supersede revision 154 暴露的 V1 不可实现 equality；这不是第四次 point-fix，而是用户确认、Design 完整修订、controller revision 156 接受后的第二次且最终 requirement redesign 实现。

唯一目标：
把 strict external setup 收敛为可证明的两阶段顺序：
external authority → existing external root-identity lease → StrictRunJournalHeaderV2 durable/readback/fsync → authenticated graceful quiesce → old PID exit + observable SQLite checkpoint/close → immutable QuiescedPreResetObservationReceiptV1 → exact whole-root snapshot/restore probe → reset/blank；pre-quiesce whole-root 只作 authorization/drift/recovery provenance，绝不能再作为 post-quiesce snapshot/restore expected hash。

必须连接的真实入口和行为：
1. DaemonSupervisor 在 ordinary ready fast-path 前识别 strict execute|recover|complete；strict launch 必须启动/等待新的 strict child 身份，不得把现有 ready daemon 当成功，也不得在 child 建立 external authority/lease/header 前 terminate/restart writer。普通非 strict ready/restart/start/stop 语义不变。
2. 在现有 daemon HTTP/token/shutdown 面上增加一个最窄、hashes-only、run-bound、幂等的 strict-quiesce request/StrictQuiesceAcceptedAckV1；request 不含路径或 secret。校验 daemon token、项目/root identity 和 canonical request hash；相同 request 返回同一 ack，冲突 request fail closed 且 writer 保持运行。accepted ack 仅证明 daemon 接受停写，不冒充 checkpoint 完成。
3. ack 后调用现有 shutdown coordinator。Bootstrap checkpoint(TRUNCATE)/DB close 失败必须对 strict owner 可观测，不能继续吞错。strict child 等待旧 PID 退出、daemon.json/daemon.pid 移除和 post-exit DB/WAL/SHM 稳态，再封印 QuiescedPreResetObservationReceiptV1。
4. receipt 必须绑定 run/authority/header/lease/root identity、pre-quiesce receipt、request/ack或typed not-running/pristine-absent、exact post-quiesce rootTreeHash、checkpoint/integrity/WAL/SHM proof、protected config/marker/public-route hashes和最小 volatile delta。只有源码证明的 daemon.json/daemon.pid 删除可默认合法；daemon log 仅在运行证据证明本次 active stdio append 时精确记录。DB/WAL/SHM、daemon-entrypoint、marker、provider config和未知路径都不能被排除或宽泛 allowlist。
5. snapshot source、restore probe、restoreWholeRootCrashSafely 和 recovery equality 全部改用 post-quiesce receipt/rootTreeHash；snapshot 后不得含 stale PID/state/token。恢复完成后只允许正常 runtime 新建 fresh daemon control identity，再恢复/reselect原 reader-mode binding并验证 DB/files/vector/route/migration/config/marker/baseline-read。
6. journal execute/recover/complete 明确并实现幂等阶段：header 前；request 未接受；accepted/正在 drain；old PID exit/checkpoint 完成但 receipt 未写；receipt 已写、snapshot 未写；snapshot/restore-probe；quarantine/reset；restore；后续 public states。fresh process 只能重放同一 request hash或从已封印 receipt/snapshot继续。timeout/conflict/malformed ack/checkpoint failure/unknown delta 均禁止 SIGKILL 后继续 snapshot/reset。
7. pristine absent 仍走 typed absent receipt，不伪造 shutdown/snapshot；authorized rebuild 使用真实 live-ready daemon 覆盖上述链。不得引入第二套 daemon control、operation lock、journal、snapshot、recovery platform，不得修改 Core/Agent/Plugin/Dashboard，不得把 Plugin 五工具或 Test 带回 pre-CAS。

验收证据：
- 先增加可复现 RED，再 GREEN。至少证明：(a) live-ready strict execute 不被吞；(b) authority→lease→header durable 时间/写入先于 request、control-file 删除和 checkpoint/root mutation；(c) malformed/conflict 不停 daemon；(d) accepted ack 幂等且 request 无路径/secret；(e) checkpoint failure 可观测并阻止 receipt/snapshot；(f) post-quiesce receipt 精确绑定 whole-root/DB/WAL/SHM和最小 delta；(g) snapshot/probe/reset成功且旧 V1 equality 不再执行；(h) 在上述每个 crash stage 由新进程恢复，exact restore 后无 stale control identity；(i) ordinary non-strict daemon ready/restart/start/stop 无回归。
- 运行 focused unit/integration tests、built-entry/live-daemon runtime probes、fresh-process crash matrix、build/typecheck/lint/仓库规则要求的完整回归。原有 26-test baseline 只是回归起点，不是完成证据。
- 两阶段自审：先逐项对 Design §§2.3/8/17/18.3 与 controller revision 156；再审认证/并发/TOCTOU、durability、checkpoint failure、hash authority、恢复幂等、普通兼容性和范围。
- 只提交 Alembic 一个干净、可审查 commit；返回 parent/commit/tree、changed files/symbols、RED/GREEN、原始命令摘要、runtime/fault evidence 路径和内容 hash、逐条契约响应、兼容性及剩余风险。
- 当前不得创建、修改、准备或派发 Test。即使本包通过，也必须由 controller 独立复验、重建并核验 exact artifacts/manifest，之后仍等待用户再次明确启动并配合 Test。 (intent: 复用 Alembic 现有 strict recovery、daemon supervisor/HTTP/shutdown、Bootstrap checkpoint、external lease/journal/snapshot，在 authority/lease/header durable 后优雅停写，并以 post-quiesce exact receipt 作为 snapshot/restore authority。)

- 2026-07-15T07:28:41.909Z decision rework (candidate tc-20260715072819-0005) — Controller two-stage review rejects Core PC-F target result.

Stage 1 / specification compliance:
1. I1 loaded-artifact reproduction is absent. The reported failing-before is only that the newly designed projectContextFoundation source did not exist; it does not execute the parent/current loaded artifact chain to reproduce or falsify the historical MR 1/5 and Plan repeatability issue, nor classify source defect versus stale dist/package/config/root identity. The runtime report loads only the post-change commit.
2. The nine-request matrix is not a valid PC-F usability proof. createProjectContextRequestAuditPlans selects one file as the only module seed per repository. The raw report has 13 MR rows and 10 SP rows with outside-scope/query-unavailable diagnostics, including internal BiliDili modules and sibling roots, yet projectContextEnvelopeToAuditResult marks them completed, readiness ignores non-empty errors, and openConfirmedDefects is derived only from readiness. Thus openConfirmedDefects=[] is unsupported and resembles a callable/single-seed probe, which Requirement §7 explicitly forbids as acceptance evidence.
3. The Core raw scanner inventory is a static import/count scan and can remain as supporting evidence, but it does not repair the missing historical reproduction or error classification.

Stage 2 / correctness and durability:
4. Controller probe reproduced a stale inventory defect: reusing one NodeProjectContextFoundationHostPorts instance after adding an eligible source file returned only the old descriptor set because enumerateEligibleFiles caches by sourceRoot+policy without revision invalidation. Complete inventory can silently omit later files.
5. Controller probe reproduced an inconsistent certification: the repo was observed git-clean, a file changed during capture, and the artifact still recorded the old clean commit/tree together with the mutated blob hash while readiness=passed. Capture never revalidates the source revision/inventory/content after reads/ProjectContext requests, so SourceRevisionVectorV1 does not certify one coherent source state.
6. The claimed private durable store/receipt/lease is not crash durable. Immutable objects are written directly to final paths with wx, so a crash can strand a partial immutable object that replay treats as a collision; lease completion uses temp+rename but neither path fsyncs the file or parent directory or verifies durable readback.

Controller reruns passed build:check, ProjectContextFoundation 10/10, public API and layer gates. These passing tests are real but do not cover the reproduced defects. The unrelated pre-existing unchanged ASTChunker doctrine failure is not added to this rework scope and must not be opportunistically fixed.

Rework must stay in AlembicCore PC-F only: add failing-before/passing-after tests for inventory invalidation, concurrent add/delete/modify and clean→dirty/dirty/content capture drift, fail-closed coherent capture, durable immutable/store/lease crash-replay semantics, and error-aware meaningful nine-request audit using complete owned module/repo scope. Regenerate a parent/loaded-artifact reproduction report plus post-fix report; every non-empty request diagnostic must be typed as expected external/advisory or remain an open defect, never silently disappear from openConfirmedDefects. No Alembic/Plugin/Agent/Dashboard edits, no I3+ Plan/Recipe work, no Ghost reset, no Test.

1. MR-ALEMBIC request audit 仍存在内部/兄弟依赖分类假阳性。最终报告把 MR 的 500 条 query-unavailable 归为 expected-external；controller 对报告逐行复核，至少 170 条明确是已认证五仓范围内的 @alembic/core、@alembic/agent 或 @alembic/plugin 依赖。scripts/audit-project-context-foundation.mjs 的 MR repositories 没有 package/module aliases，Node classifier 只对 declared module basename 或 approved portable alias 识别 internal/sibling，导致同仓 self-package imports 和跨仓 imports 被错误标为“outside certified ownership”。因此 request usable/ownership graph 尚未证明，openConfirmedDefects=[] 不可信。修复必须基于真实 package exports/aliases 与 canonical repo/module ownership正确分类并解析，而不是把所有 @alembic/* 机械改成 advisory；任何仍未解析的当前仓内部模块应是 confirmed defect，已批准兄弟仓应携带 relatedRepoId，对真正第三方才允许 expected-external。重生成 MR/SP 报告并逐类守恒。

2. capture 仍能认证混合源码状态。captureRepositories 在文件读取前只调用一次 observeRevision；assertSourceStateStable 只是再执行同样的“先观察、后读取”流程。controller 用当前 dist 的 public foundation API 构造确定性并发变更探针：两轮 observe 均返回同一 git-clean commit/tree，但每轮 read 期间切到相同 dirty 新内容；最终 source 状态为 dirty，artifact 却以 git-clean revision + dirty blob、readiness=passed 返回。修复必须让每次 snapshot 的 revision/content 形成真正的闭区间认证（至少读取前后 revision/content fencing，并能拒绝 transient clean→dirty→clean/dirty-new 组合；clean revision 的内容必须与所声明 tree一致），为本 controller probe 添加 RED/GREEN 回归。不得依赖“再跑一次相同的 pre-read observation”作为终态屏障。

修复范围严格限定上述两点及其报告/测试；不重做已通过的 durability/cache 设计，不修 ASTChunker，不修改其他仓，不进入 I3+、Alembic/Plugin 下游或 Test。新的证据必须包含 updated repair commit、MR internal/sibling/external diagnostic counts与样例、openConfirmedDefects derivation、source-state adversarial probe、focused/full gates和两阶段自审。

Stage 1 / specification compliance:

- Root cause B is not closed for the contract's content-revision host path. In capture.ts verifyCapturedSnapshot(), a host with revision.kind=content and no optional verifySnapshot receives verified=true without a terminal full-content reread inside the round. The second whole capture is not a closed fence: the controller's deterministic black-box probe reset to clean at each enumerate, changed to the same dirty bytes at each read, returned kind=content throughout, and finished with accepted=true, readiness=passed, enumerateCount=2, readCount=2, finalConceptualState=dirty. This violates the package requirement that dirty/content modes bind the final full-content hash to a stable terminal observation and that capture certify one coherent state.
- The committed test 'keeps legacy content hosts compatible through two matching complete candidates' proves only stable input; it does not exercise the repeated same-mutation pattern and therefore masks this gap.

Stage 2 / quality:
- Controller reruns passed npm run build:check, ProjectContextFoundation 37/37, and git diff --check. These are valid but demonstrate a test-coverage gap, not correctness of the content snapshot protocol.
- The git verifier and ownership work may be preserved. No new defect is asserted against the accepted cache/store durability portions.

The original task is already recurringProblem=true and this root-cause re-derivation package explicitly said that if it still failed, the same point-fix lane must stop. Therefore the controller marks this review blocked instead of dispatching another ordinary rework. No Alembic/Plugin adapter, I3+, or Test work is unlocked. The blocker is a compatibility/protocol decision and fresh implementation hypothesis: either require a verifier for every revision kind with an explicit versioned migration, or implement an in-Core terminal full-content reread/fence for legacy content hosts; in both cases the exact controller content-mode probe must fail with PROJECT_CONTEXT_SOURCE_STATE_DRIFT before review can resume.

Root cause: CertifiedProjectFactsRuntime eagerly reopens once in a central loop and materializes all four consumer projections/receipts with the same entrypoint, then serializes four complete copies into the session carrier. The actual plan, recipe-generation, dependency-graph, and module-coverage entrypoints consume prebuilt projections, so the receipts do not prove four actual loaded adapters. Independent ModuleService probe found providerCalls=1: after an initial legacy load, a later certified carrier was never read because #loaded short-circuits, so strict execution can silently remain on the legacy path. The four zero counters and legacy unreachability rows are constants, not runtime/reachability measurements. The >12 files/>80 modules test fabricates 85 modules while 72 own no files; the ProjectScope test creates five child repositories rather than the control root plus four members; neither proves the required real conservation/topology. Repair only Alembic Main I2.1: keep Core unchanged; make actual consumers reopen/use one immutable artifact with truthful entrypoint receipts, a bounded durable carrier, correct relativeRoot-based paths, fail-closed ModuleService rebinding, and measured bypass/cap/synthesis evidence. Replace synthetic acceptance fixtures with captured >12/>80 ownership conservation and exact root+4 topology, and regenerate evidence from actual runs. Do not start Plugin/GraphMap, I3+, Test, or cold-start reset redesign in this repair.

Therefore every strict authorized-rebuild (existing dataRoot) still executes ensureDaemonDirs(paths), enters the target-local supervisor lock, opens target-local daemon.log, and later writes pid before spawning the child that acquires the external root-identity lease, writes/fsyncs StrictRunJournalHeader, and snapshots pre-reset state. The new supervisor test covers only pristine and hand-simulates the child's external lease/header/init; it cannot detect rebuild pre-mutation. Root-cause hypothesis for the next same-task repair: strict launch safety was modeled as a target-absence special case, while the supervisor remains a target-local lifecycle writer before the child. Repair the unified strict external launch boundary for both execute modes: no target-local daemon directory/log/lock/pid/state mutation before child authority establishment; retain ordinary non-strict behavior. Add executable rebuild regression that starts from an existing sentinel root and proves byte/path inventory unchanged at spawn and until external lease+header+snapshot authority, plus a real entry seam for recover/complete. However DaemonSupervisor.start performs the generic `existing.ready && !restart` return before it even computes strictExternalLaunch. A real ready-daemon probe with ALEMBIC_STRICT_SETUP_AUTHORITY_PATH present returned ready, did not spawn the strict child, and left the old writer alive; the execute/recover/complete action was silently skipped. With restart=true, a second real-process probe showed the supervisor terminated the old writer before child spawn while external operation-lock and strict journal paths were still absent. This violates the confirmed fixed order in Requirement Design §8.2/line 452 and §13.1/line 906: R0 external root-identity lock + durable journal precede quiesce, snapshot, reset, restore and mutation. It also leaves the original task package's live-owner/fresh-process entry requirement unproved. This is not a third condition tweak: the new root-cause hypothesis is that generic daemon lifecycle owns ready short-circuit and process termination before strict authority classification, while strict R0 must own action dispatch and quiescence. Re-derive the entry boundary so strict authority is classified before generic ready handling; a strict action can never be treated as ordinary already-ready success. Quiesce of an existing writer must happen only after the child has validated authority, acquired the external operation lease, fsynced the journal header, and signaled a typed authority-established/quiesce handoff, without adding a second orchestrator or arbitrary path API. Add executable ready-daemon tests for restart=false action execution and restart=true/live-owner ordering, plus malformed/conflicting authority proving the old writer is not stopped. Preserve commit 43306622's zero target-root mutation and real built-entry recovery/completion tests. Test remains paused.

SemanticDispositionReviewExecution.ts:490-507 branches around validateProducerAdmissionContext when proposal.expression is null, and producerZeroTargetInvalid at 586-596 requires admissionReceipt === null; StrictProductionAuthority then has no zero-disposition Admission consumption. This makes reviewed-non-draft a privileged bypass and leaves zero-output suppression without the exact Admission authority explicitly required by the original completion definition. Second, the purported anti-stamping host lineage is still structurally self-attested: promptHash at 294-300 hashes a Core semantic request object, not the exact prompt bytes actually sent; createAgentSemanticDispositionReviewExecutionV1 accepts a caller-supplied ordinary invocation object and derives reviewer identity from its copied fields; validateInvocation only compares those fields back to request/calibration. The focused fixture at test/StrictDispositionReviewExecutionAuthority.test.ts:790-841 locally creates a passing decision, JSON.stringify(response), copies provider/model/load/request/prompt values, and labels arbitrary run/invocation IDs—there is no Agent host call, actual prompt transcript/compiled-prompt receipt, or non-rebindable host execution record. Its evidence fixture at 477-499 is also a caller-authored summary whose blobHash/entry/session/subject are not joined to an authoritative frozen Evidence Ledger/blob receipt; normalizeEvidence only self-hashes content. Therefore the advertised negative probe does not reject the original failure mode of an unrelated/generic call followed by matching-field stamping, and 17/17 passing tests are a false green for this claim. Rework in the same Core window: (1) make mandatory zero/non-draft carry and consume the exact G1/Admission/corpus-inspection chain with a reviewable fingerprint, and add positive/negative terminal conservation tests; (2) version the request/execution contract so it binds exact compiled prompt bytes/hash and an Agent-owned host execution/load/result record that Main can only consume, with strict authority exact-one/non-replay joins; (3) bind every evidence row to authoritative frozen Evidence Ledger/blob/source/fact coordinates and reject summary-only or rebound evidence; (4) add a real negative fixture reproducing generic/unrelated response + post-hoc matching-field stamping and prove it fails. Preserve correct workflow/evaluator run separation and existing merge/duplicate/investigated-empty lineage.

(1) src/production.ts publicly export-stars SemanticDispositionReviewExecution, including createAgentSemanticDispositionReviewHostGatewayV2. That public mint accepts any caller-supplied adapter.invoke and registers its returned pass JSON in a process-local WeakMap. The focused success fixture at test lines 1213-1237 uses exactly such an in-memory adapter: it constructs passingDecisionV2 locally and JSON.stringify returns it, with no Agent runtime/provider/load call. Therefore a Main-side caller can mint a new registered authority around arbitrary output; the unrelated-record restamp negative only rejects reuse under an existing authority and does not test or reject this public mint path. A comment saying only Agent calls the function does not establish Agent ownership. (2) The WeakMap authority at source lines 770-804 is non-serializable and disappears in a fresh process, while createStrictProductionAuthorityReceiptV1 lines 281-284 claims cross-process persistent authority and the requirement mandates durable journal/fresh-process recovery. prior hashes do not recreate or independently verify the host call. (3) Evidence authority remains internally consistent but not authoritative: createSemanticDispositionReviewEvidenceAuthorityV2 accepts ordinary caller-provided EvidenceEntry/snapshot/witness/execution objects, and the test fixture at lines 1383 onward fabricates all of them with local strings and hashes. It proves rebind detection but no frozen Evidence Ledger/store/blob/fact load authority. Required repair: preserve the zero Admission fix; move authority mint/verification behind a non-public Agent-owned adapter/attestation boundary that Main cannot mint, define a durable fresh-process-verifiable execution receipt or verifier rehydration path, and bind evidence to an authoritative frozen store/load receipt or opaque verifier rather than only self-consistent objects. Add explicit negatives proving a Main/public-facade caller cannot create a fake passing gateway, a serialized/restarted authority validates only through the approved durable verifier and not WeakMap memory, and a wholly self-built ledger/blob/fact object graph is rejected. build:check, focused 21/21, diff check and clean commit all passed but are insufficient because the passing fixture itself exercises the fake adapter path.

Thus one malformed public call can durably poison the authority, violating the task package's controlled-capture and corrupt-JSONL fail-closed completion definition. Rework must add side-effect-free runtime validation before sequence allocation/append/write (including valid EvidenceToolId, valid 1-based range, required string/value shape and boundary policy), reject malformed drafts with a stable Agent production error, prove the JSONL and sequence remain unchanged after every rejected input, then rerun focused authority/runtime tests, fresh-process public probe, full check, and diff check. Do not broaden into Main/Core or a second store; the existing downstream Main task remains blocked on an accepted Agent authority.
